   28: function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
   29:   return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
   30:     ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)
   31:     : {};
   32: }

   34: function asEvidenceItems(value: unknown): RuntimeCaseEvidenceItems {
   35:   if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
   36:     return [] as RuntimeCaseEvidenceItems;
   37:   }
   38: 
   39:   const items = value
   40:     .filter((item): item is Record<string, unknown> => {
   41:       return Boolean(item && typeof item === "object" && !Array.isArray(item));
   42:     })
   43:     .map((item) => ({
   44:       id: String(item.id || randomUUID()),
   45:       fileName: String(item.fileName || "شاهد"),
   46:       fileUrl: String(item.fileUrl || ""),
   47:       mimeType: String(item.mimeType || "application/octet-stream"),
   48:       size: Number(item.size || 0),
   49:     }))
   50:     .filter((item) => item.fileUrl);
   51: 
   52:   return items as RuntimeCaseEvidenceItems;
   53: }

   57: 
   58:   if (!text || text === "undefined" || text === "null" || text.length > 140) {
   59:     return "";
   60:   }
   61: 
   62:   return text;
   63: }

   39: function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
   40:   return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
   41:     ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)
   42:     : {};
   43: }

   45: function asEvidenceItems(value: unknown) {
   46:   if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
   47: 
   48:   return value
   49:     .filter((item): item is Record<string, unknown> => {
   50:       return Boolean(item && typeof item === "object" && !Array.isArray(item));
   51:     })
   52:     .map((item) => ({
   53:       id: String(item.id || ""),
   54:       fileName: String(item.fileName || "شاهد"),
   55:       fileUrl: String(item.fileUrl || ""),
   56:       mimeType: String(item.mimeType || "application/octet-stream"),
   57:       size: Number(item.size || 0),
   58:     }))
   59:     .filter((item) => item.fileUrl);
   60: }

   18: function hasValidImageSignature(buffer: Buffer, mimeType: PortfolioImageMimeType) {
   19:   if (mimeType === "image/jpeg") return buffer.length >= 3 && buffer[0] === 0xff && buffer[1] === 0xd8 && buffer[2] === 0xff;
   20:   if (mimeType === "image/png") return buffer.length >= 8 && buffer.subarray(0, 8).equals(Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a]));
   21:   return buffer.length >= 12 && buffer.subarray(0, 4).toString("ascii") === "RIFF" && buffer.subarray(8, 12).toString("ascii") === "WEBP";
   22: }

   59:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
   60:     if (Array.isArray(candidate)) {
   61:       collected.push(...candidate);
   62:       continue;
   63:     }
   64: 
   65:     if (candidate && typeof candidate === "object") {
   66:       if (Array.isArray(candidate.items)) {
   67:         collected.push(...candidate.items);
   68:       }
   69: 
   70:       if (Array.isArray(candidate.evidences)) {
   71:         collected.push(...candidate.evidences);
   72:       }
   73:     }
   74:   }
   75: 
   76:   const seen = new Set<string>();
   77: 
   78:   return collected.filter((item, index) => {
   79:     const fileUrl =
   80:       item.fileUrl ||
   81:       item.url ||
   82:       item.imageUrl ||
   83:       item.publicUrl ||
   84:       item.path ||
   85:       "";
   86: 
   87:     const signature = `${item.id || index}-${fileUrl}`;
   88: 
   89:     if (!fileUrl || seen.has(signature)) return false;
   90: 
   91:     seen.add(signature);
   92:     return true;
   93:   });
   94: }

   71: export function formatCertificateDate(value: Date | string) {
   72:   const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
   73: 
   74:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
   75:     return "";
   76:   }
   77: 
   78:   return date.toLocaleDateString("ar-SA-u-ca-gregory", {
   79:     year: "numeric",
   80:     month: "2-digit",
   81:     day: "2-digit",
   82:   });

  404:       <h1 class="title">${escapeHtml(certificate.title || "شهادة")}</h1>

  407:       <div class="name">${escapeHtml(certificate.recipientName)}</div>

  432:   </section>
  433: </body>

    1: import "server-only";
    2: 
    3: import {
    4:   deleteEvidenceFile,
    5:   getEvidenceStoredFileNameFromUrl,
    6: } from "@/lib/evidence/evidence-file-storage";
    7: 
    8: export async function deleteEvidenceFiles(fileUrls: Array<string | null>) {
    9:   const uniqueFileNames = Array.from(new Set(
   10:     fileUrls
   11:       .map((fileUrl) => fileUrl ? getEvidenceStoredFileNameFromUrl(fileUrl) : null)
   12:       .filter((fileName): fileName is string => Boolean(fileName)),
   13:   ));
   14: 
   15:   const results = await Promise.all(uniqueFileNames.map((fileName) => deleteEvidenceFile(fileName)));
   16:   return results.reduce((total, failures) => total + failures, 0);
   17: }

   27:   if (!fileName || fileName === "." || fileName === ".." || fileName.includes("\0")) {
   28:     return false;
   29:   }
   30:

   32:     path.basename(fileName) !== fileName ||

FILE: D:\student-guidance-platform-clean\lib\evidence\evidence-file-storage.ts

   55: async function isRegularFile(filePath: string) {
   56:   try {
   57:     return (await stat(filePath)).isFile();
   58:   } catch {
   59:     return false;
   60:   }
   61: }
   62: 
   63: export async function resolveExistingEvidenceFile(fileName: string) {
   64:   if (!isSafeEvidenceStoredFileName(fileName)) return null;
   65: 
   66:   for (const root of [getEvidencePersistentStorageRoot(), getEvidenceLegacyStorageRoot()]) {
   67:     const filePath = resolveEvidenceFilePath(root, fileName);
   68:     if (filePath && await isRegularFile(filePath)) return filePath;
   69:   }
   70: 
   71:   return null;
   72: }
   73: 
   74: export async function writeEvidenceFile(fileName: string, data: Uint8Array) {
   75:   const root = getEvidencePersistentStorageRoot();
   76:   const filePath = resolveEvidenceFilePath(root, fileName);
   77:   if (!filePath) throw new Error("Invalid evidence file name.");
   78: 
   79:   await mkdir(root, { recursive: true });
   80:   await writeFile(filePath, data, { flag: "wx" });
   81:   return filePath;
   82: }
   83: 
   84: export async function deleteEvidenceFile(fileName: string) {
   85:   if (!isSafeEvidenceStoredFileName(fileName)) return 0;
   86: 
   87:   let failures = 0;
   88:   for (const root of [getEvidencePersistentStorageRoot(), getEvidenceLegacyStorageRoot()]) {
   89:     const filePath = resolveEvidenceFilePath(root, fileName);
   90:     if (!filePath) continue;
   91: 
   92:     try {
   93:       await unlink(filePath);
   94:     } catch (error) {

   24: function sanitizeOriginalFileName(fileName: string) {
   25:   const withoutPosixPath = path.posix.basename(String(fileName || ""));
   26:   const withoutWindowsPath = path.win32.basename(withoutPosixPath);
   27:   const normalized = withoutWindowsPath
   28:     .replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/g, "")
   29:     .replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*]+/g, "-")
   30:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   31:     .trim();
   32: 
   33:   return normalized.slice(0, 180) || "file";
   34: }
   35: 
   36: function getSafeExtension(file: File) {
   37:   const safeName = sanitizeOriginalFileName(file.name);
   38:   const rawExtension = path.extname(safeName).toLowerCase().replace(/^\./, "");
   39:   const allowedExtensions =
   40:     ALLOWED_EVIDENCE_TYPES[file.type as keyof typeof ALLOWED_EVIDENCE_TYPES];
   41: 
   42:   if (!allowedExtensions || !rawExtension) {
   43:     return null;
   44:   }

   95: export async function saveEvidenceFiles(params: {
   96:   files: File[];
   97:   schoolAccountId: string;
   98: }) {
   99:   const uploadedItems: SavedEvidenceFile[] = [];
  100: 
  101:   for (const file of params.files) {
  102:     const extension = getSafeExtension(file);
  103: 
  104:     if (!extension) {
  105:       continue;
  106:     }
  107: 
  108:     const safeOriginalName = sanitizeOriginalFileName(file.name);
  109:     const storedName = `${params.schoolAccountId}-${crypto.randomUUID()}.${extension}`;
  110:     const publicUrl = `/uploads/evidence/${storedName}`;
  111: 
  112:     const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
  113:     await writeEvidenceFile(storedName, buffer);
  114: 
  115:     uploadedItems.push({
  116:       fileName: safeOriginalName,
  117:       fileUrl: publicUrl,
  118:       mimeType: file.type,
  119:       size: file.size,
  120:     });
  121:   }
  122: 
  123:   return uploadedItems;
  124: }

   99: function cleanText(value: unknown) {
  100:   const text = String(repairPotentialUtf8Mojibake(value) ?? "").trim();
  101: 
  102:   return text && text !== "null" && text !== "undefined" ? text : "";
  103: }
  104: 
  105: function normalizeInternalSignatureUrl(value: unknown) {
  106:   const url = cleanText(value).replace(/\\/g, "/");
  107: 
  108:   if (!url) return "";
  109:   if (url.includes("..")) return "";
  110:   if (/^[a-z]:\//i.test(url)) return "";
  111:   if (/^(https?:|data:|javascript:|file:|blob:)/i.test(url)) return "";
  112:   if (!url.startsWith("/uploads/school-signatures/")) return "";
  113: 
  114:   return url;
  115: }
  116: 
  117: function normalizeText(value: unknown) {
  118:   return cleanText(value)
  119:     .toLowerCase()
  120:     .replace(/[أإآ]/g, "ا")
  121:     .replace(/ى/g, "ي")
  122:     .replace(/ة/g, "ه")
  123:     .replace(/[_\s-]+/g, "")
  124:     .trim();
  125: }
  126: 
  127: function normalizeLookupKey(value: unknown) {

  130:     .replace(/[أإآ]/g, "ا")

   71:   ]);

   83:     results.push({ sourceId: report.id, sourceType: "REPORT_SNAPSHOT", sectionId: section.id, sectionKey: section.key, title: content.title || report.reportTitle, serviceName: content.serviceName || report.serviceName || "تقرير", caseTitle: null, status: report.status, generatedAt: (report.approvedAt || report.savedAt).toISOString(), createdAt: report.createdAt.toISOString(), previewUrl: `/dashboard/report-2/snapshots/${report.id}/preview`, content });
   84:   }
   85:   const activeIds = new Set(active.map((item) => item.id));
   86:   for (const report of snapshots) {
   87:     if (activeIds.has(report.id)) continue;
   88:     const section = report.serviceSlug ? sectionBySlug.get(report.serviceSlug) : null; if (!section) continue;

   92:   return results;

   99: export function manageEvidence(content: PortfolioReportContent, metadata: unknown): PortfolioManagedEvidence[] {
  100:   const preferences = evidencePreferences(metadata);
  101:   return content.evidenceItems.map((item, index) => {

  194: export async function updatePortfolioEvidencePreference(user: PortfolioActor, portfolioId: string, itemId: string, evidenceId: string, patch: Partial<PortfolioEvidencePreference>) {
  195:   const { item, report } = await reportAndEvidence(user, portfolioId, itemId, evidenceId);
  196:   const meta = object(item.metadataJson); const prefs = evidencePreferences(meta); const current = object(prefs[evidenceId]);
  197:   const defaultOrder = (report.content.evidenceItems.findIndex((entry) => entry.id === evidenceId) + 1) * 10;
  198:   prefs[evidenceId] = { isVisible: current.isVisible !== false, sortOrder: typeof current.sortOrder === "number" ? current.sortOrder : defaultOrder, customTitle: typeof current.customTitle === "string" ? current.customTitle : "", customDescription: typeof current.customDescription === "string" ? current.customDescription : "", ...patch };
  199:   await prisma.achievementPortfolioItem.update({ where: { id: item.id }, data: { metadataJson: json({ ...meta, evidencePreferences: prefs }) } });
  200: }
  201: 
  202: export async function movePortfolioEvidence(user: PortfolioActor, portfolioId: string, itemId: string, evidenceId: string, direction: "up" | "down") {
  203:   const { item, report } = await reportAndEvidence(user, portfolioId, itemId, evidenceId);
  204:   const managed = manageEvidence(report.content, item.metadataJson); const index = managed.findIndex((entry) => entry.id === evidenceId); const target = direction === "up" ? index - 1 : index + 1;
  205:   if (target < 0 || target >= managed.length) return;
  206:   [managed[index], managed[target]] = [managed[target], managed[index]];
  207:   const meta = object(item.metadataJson); const prefs = evidencePreferences(meta);
  208:   managed.forEach((entry, order) => { const current = object(prefs[entry.id]); prefs[entry.id] = { ...current, isVisible: entry.isVisible, sortOrder: (order + 1) * 10, customTitle: entry.title === entry.originalTitle ? "" : entry.title, customDescription: entry.description }; });
  209:   await prisma.achievementPortfolioItem.update({ where: { id: item.id }, data: { metadataJson: json({ ...meta, evidencePreferences: prefs }) } });
  210: }

  212: export async function loadCustomEvidence(user: PortfolioActor, portfolioId: string): Promise<PortfolioCustomEvidence[]> {
  213:   await requireOwnedPortfolio(user, portfolioId);
  214:   const items = await prisma.achievementPortfolioItem.findMany({ where: { portfolioId, sourceType: "CUSTOM_EVIDENCE" }, orderBy: [{ sortOrder: "asc" }, { createdAt: "asc" }] });
  215:   return items.map((item) => { const meta = object(item.metadataJson); return { id: item.id, sectionId: item.sectionId, title: item.title, description: item.description || "", fileUrl: typeof meta.fileUrl === "string" ? meta.fileUrl : "", mimeType: typeof meta.mimeType === "string" ? meta.mimeType : "", sortOrder: item.sortOrder, isVisible: item.isVisible }; });
  216: }

  218: async function validateCustomSection(portfolioId: string, sectionId?: string | null) {
  219:   if (!sectionId) return null;
  220:   const section = await prisma.achievementPortfolioSection.findFirst({ where: { id: sectionId, portfolioId } });
  221:   if (!section) throw new PortfolioServiceError(404, "القسم المحدد غير موجود."); return section.id;

  224: export async function createCustomEvidence(user: PortfolioActor, portfolioId: string, input: { title: string; description: string; fileUrl: string; mimeType: string; sectionId?: string | null; isVisible: boolean }) {
  225:   await requireOwnedPortfolio(user, portfolioId); const sectionId = await validateCustomSection(portfolioId, input.sectionId);
  226:   const last = await prisma.achievementPortfolioItem.findFirst({ where: { portfolioId, sourceType: "CUSTOM_EVIDENCE" }, orderBy: { sortOrder: "desc" } });
  227:   return prisma.achievementPortfolioItem.create({ data: { portfolioId, sectionId, sourceType: "CUSTOM_EVIDENCE", title: input.title, description: input.description || null, isVisible: input.isVisible, sortOrder: (last?.sortOrder || 0) + 10, metadataJson: json({ fileUrl: input.fileUrl, mimeType: input.mimeType }) } });
  228: }

  230: async function customItem(user: PortfolioActor, portfolioId: string, itemId: string) { await requireOwnedPortfolio(user, portfolioId); const item = await prisma.achievementPortfolioItem.findFirst({ where: { id: itemId, portfolioId, sourceType: "CUSTOM_EVIDENCE" } }); if (!item) throw new PortfolioServiceError(404, "الشاهد المستقل غير موجود."); return item; }
  231: export async function updateCustomEvidence(user: PortfolioActor, portfolioId: string, itemId: string, input: Partial<{ title: string; description: string; fileUrl: string; mimeType: string; sectionId: string | null; isVisible: boolean }>) { const item = await customItem(user, portfolioId, itemId); const sectionId = input.sectionId === undefined ? item.sectionId : await validateCustomSection(portfolioId, input.sectionId); const meta = object(item.metadataJson); return prisma.achievementPortfolioItem.update({ where: { id: item.id }, data: { sectionId, title: input.title ?? item.title, description: input.description ?? item.description, isVisible: input.isVisible ?? item.isVisible, metadataJson: json({ ...meta, fileUrl: input.fileUrl ?? meta.fileUrl ?? "", mimeType: input.mimeType ?? meta.mimeType ?? "" }) } }); }

  233: export async function moveCustomEvidence(user: PortfolioActor, portfolioId: string, itemId: string, direction: "up" | "down") { const item = await customItem(user, portfolioId, itemId); const items = await prisma.achievementPortfolioItem.findMany({ where: { portfolioId, sourceType: "CUSTOM_EVIDENCE" }, orderBy: [{ sortOrder: "asc" }, { createdAt: "asc" }] }); const index = items.findIndex((entry) => entry.id === item.id); const target = direction === "up" ? index - 1 : index + 1; if (target < 0 || target >= items.length) return; [items[index], items[target]] = [items[target], items[index]]; await prisma.$transaction(items.map((entry, order) => prisma.achievementPortfolioItem.update({ where: { id: entry.id }, data: { sortOrder: (order + 1) * 10 } }))); }

   42: export function createReferenceLibraryStorageKey(extension: string) {
   43:   const date = new Date();
   44:   const year = String(date.getUTCFullYear());
   45:   const month = String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
   46:   const safeExtension = extension.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
   47: 
   48:   if (!safeExtension) {
   49:     throw new Error("INVALID_FILE_EXTENSION");
   50:   }
   51: 
   52:   return `${year}/${month}/${randomUUID()}.${safeExtension}`;
   53: }
   54: 
   55: export async function saveReferenceLibraryFile(input: {
   56:   storageKey: string;
   57:   buffer: Uint8Array;
   58: }) {
   59:   const absolutePath = resolveStoragePath(input.storageKey);
   60: 
   61:   await mkdir(path.dirname(absolutePath), {
   62:     recursive: true,
   63:   });
   64: 
   65:   await writeFile(absolutePath, input.buffer);
   66: 
   67:   return {
   68:     storageKey: input.storageKey,
   69:     sizeBytes: input.buffer.byteLength,
   70:   };
   71: }
   72: 
   73: export async function readReferenceLibraryFile(storageKey: string) {
   74:   const absolutePath = resolveStoragePath(storageKey);
   75:   return readFile(absolutePath);
   76: }
   77: 
   78: export async function referenceLibraryFileExists(storageKey: string) {
   79:   try {
   80:     const absolutePath = resolveStoragePath(storageKey);
   81:     const fileStat = await stat(absolutePath);
   82:     return fileStat.isFile();
   83:   } catch {
   84:     return false;
   85:   }
   86: }

  122:     return "";
  123:   }

  126:     return value;
  127:   }
  128:

  130:     return String(value);

  147:   /\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif|avif)(?:[?#].*)?$/i;

  149: function hasReportImageEvidenceExtension(value: unknown) {
  150:   const text = String(value || "").trim().replaceAll("\\", "/");

  152:   if (!text) {

  154:   }

  159: function isImageEvidence(item: {
  160:   type?: string | null;
  161:   mimeType?: string | null;
  162:   fileUrl?: string | null;
  163:   url?: string | null;
  164:   imageUrl?: string | null;

 1033: function buildSignatures(
 1034:   caseEntry: any,
 1035:   current: CurrentUserLike,
 1036:   languageMode: ReportLanguageMode,
 1037: ): SmartReportSignature[] {
 1038:   const profile = resolveSchoolProfileForReport(caseEntry, current);
 1039:   const serviceSlug = caseEntry.service?.slug || "";
 1040:   const isActivity = isActivityProgramReportService(serviceSlug);
 1041:   const hasActivityAssignment = Boolean(caseEntry.activityAssignment);
 1042:   const transformTitle = (value: string) =>
 1043:     applyReportLanguageModeToText(value, languageMode);
 1044: 
 1045:   const signatures: SmartReportSignature[] = [];
 1046: 
 1047:   signatures.push({
 1048:     key: "principal",
 1049:     label: transformTitle("مدير المدرسة"),
 1050:     signerName: profile?.principalName || "مدير المدرسة",
 1051:     signerTitle: transformTitle("مدير المدرسة"),
 1052:     imageUrl: profile?.principalSignatureUrl || null,
 1053:     required: false,
 1054:   });
 1055: 
 1056:   if (
 1057:     isActivity &&
 1058:     (current.user.role !== "TEACHER" || hasActivityAssignment)
 1059:   ) {
 1060:     signatures.push({
 1061:       key: "activity_leader",
 1062:       label: transformTitle("رائد النشاط"),
 1063:       signerName:
 1064:         profile?.activityLeaderName ||
 1065:         current.user.officialName ||

   89: export async function writeSchoolSignatureFile(
   90:   schoolAccountId: string,
   91:   fileName: string,
   92:   data: Uint8Array,
   93: ) {
   94:   const root = getSchoolSignaturePersistentStorageRoot();
   95:   const ownerDirectory = resolveOwnerDirectory(root, schoolAccountId);
   96: 
   97:   if (!ownerDirectory) {
   98:     throw new Error("Invalid school account id.");
   99:   }
  100: 
  101:   const filePath = resolveSignatureFilePath(
  102:     root,
  103:     schoolAccountId,
  104:     fileName,
  105:   );
  106: 
  107:   if (!filePath) {
  108:     throw new Error("Invalid school signature file name.");
  109:   }
  110: 
  111:   await mkdir(ownerDirectory, { recursive: true });
  112:   await writeFile(filePath, data, { flag: "wx" });
  113: 
  114:   return filePath;
  115: }

  135:       root,
  136:       schoolAccountId,
  137:       fileName,

    1: import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
    2: import { mkdir, readFile, rm, stat, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
    3: import path from "node:path";
    4: 
    5: const STORAGE_ROOT = path.resolve(process.cwd(), ".storage", "workflows");
    6: const EXCEL_EXTENSIONS = new Set(["xlsx", "xls"]);
    7: 
    8: function cleanSegment(value: string) {
    9:   const cleaned = value.trim().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "-");
   10:   if (!cleaned) throw new Error("INVALID_STORAGE_SEGMENT");
   11:   return cleaned;
   12: }
   13: 
   14: function resolveStoragePath(storageKey: string) {
   15:   const normalizedKey = storageKey.replace(/\\/g, "/").replace(/^\/+/, "");
   16:   if (!normalizedKey || normalizedKey.includes("../") || normalizedKey.includes("..\\")) {
   17:     throw new Error("INVALID_STORAGE_KEY");
   18:   }
   19: 
   20:   const absolutePath = path.resolve(STORAGE_ROOT, normalizedKey);
   21:   const relativePath = path.relative(STORAGE_ROOT, absolutePath);
   22:   if (relativePath.startsWith("..") || path.isAbsolute(relativePath)) {
   23:     throw new Error("INVALID_STORAGE_KEY");
   24:   }
   25: 
   26:   return absolutePath;
   27: }
   28:

FILE: D:\student-guidance-platform-clean\lib\storage\workflow-original-file-storage.ts
MATCH LINE: 50
============================================================
   26:   return absolutePath;
   27: }
   28: 
   29: export function getWorkflowExcelExtension(fileName: string) {
   30:   const extension = path.extname(fileName).slice(1).toLowerCase();
   31:   return EXCEL_EXTENSIONS.has(extension) ? extension : null;
   32: }
   33: 
   34: export function createWorkflowOriginalFileStorageKey(input: {
   35:   serviceSlug: string;
   36:   workflowId: string;
   37:   extension: string;
   38: }) {
   39:   const extension = input.extension.toLowerCase();
   40:   if (!EXCEL_EXTENSIONS.has(extension)) throw new Error("INVALID_EXCEL_EXTENSION");
   41: 
   42:   return `${cleanSegment(input.serviceSlug)}/${cleanSegment(input.workflowId)}/${randomUUID()}.${extension}`;
   43: }
   44: 
   45: export async function saveWorkflowOriginalFile(input: {
   46:   storageKey: string;
   47:   buffer: Uint8Array;
   48: }) {
   49:   const absolutePath = resolveStoragePath(input.storageKey);
   50:   await mkdir(path.dirname(absolutePath), { recursive: true });
   51:   await writeFile(absolutePath, input.buffer);
   52: }
   53: 
   54: export async function readWorkflowOriginalFile(storageKey: string) {
   55:   return readFile(resolveStoragePath(storageKey));
   56: }
   57: 
   58: export async function workflowOriginalFileExists(storageKey: string) {
   59:   try {
   60:     return (await stat(resolveStoragePath(storageKey))).isFile();
   61:   } catch {
   62:     return false;
   63:   }
   64: }
   65: 
   66: export async function deleteWorkflowOriginalFile(storageKey?: string | null) {
   67:   if (!storageKey) return;
   68:   await rm(resolveStoragePath(storageKey), { force: true });
   69: }
